           │         pass                                                 │

           │                                                              │

           │         """                                                  │
           │         pass                                                 │
           │                                                              │

           │         """(Optional) Defines the local variables and their  │

           │         For example, the following implementation defines an │
           │ 8-bit variable x                                             │
           │         and an integer variable y:                           │

           │         ```                                                  │
           │         """                                                  │
           │         pass                                                 │
           │                                                              │

           │         """(Optional) Defines the input variables and their  │
           │ types.                                                       │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines an │
           │ input variable x,                                            │
           │         which is an array of 8-bit bitvectors indexed by     │
           │ 2-bit bitvectors:                                            │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         def inputs(self):                                    │
           │             self.x = Array(BitVector(2), BitVector(8))       │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         """                                                  │
           │         pass                                                 │
           │                                                              │
           │     def outputs(self):                                       │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the output variables and their │
           │ types.                                                       │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines an │
           │ output variable y,                                           │
           │         which is a real number:                              │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         def outputs(self):                                   │
           │             self.y = Real()                                  │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         """                                                  │
           │         pass                                                 │
           │                                                              │
           │     def shared_vars(self):                                   │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the shared variables and their │

           │         which is an array of booleans indexed by integers:   │

           │         def shared_vars(self):                               │
           │             self.z = Array(Integer(), Boolean())             │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         """                                                  │
           │         pass                                                 │
           │                                                              │

           │         implementation defines an instance of M called m,    │

           │         input variable x to the local variable self.a, M's   │

           │         the local variable self.b, and M's output variable z │

           │         variable self.c:                                     │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         def instances(self):                                 │
           │             self.m = M(x=self.a, y=self.b, z=self.c)         │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         """                                                  │
           │         pass                                                 │
           │                                                              │
           │     def init(self):                                          │

           │ initializes x to 0 if y is                                   │
           │         greater than or equal to 20:                         │
           │         ```                                                  │

           │         ```                                                  │

           │         pass                                                 │
           │                                                              │

           │             if self.y >= 20:                                 │

           │         ```                                                  │
           │         """                                                  │
           │         pass                                                 │

           │         """(Optional) Defines the transition relation.       │
           │         For example, the following implementation increments │
           │ x or decrements y                                            │
           │         depending on z:                                      │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         def next(self):                                      │
           │             if self.z:                                       │

           │         ```                                                  │
           │         """                                                  │
           │         pass                                                 │
           │                                                              │

           │         """(Optional) Defines the local variables and their  │

           │ 8-bit variable x                                             │
           │         and an integer variable y:                           │

           │         """                                                  │
           │         pass                                                 │
           │                                                              │

           │         For example, the following implementation defines an │

           │         which is an array of 8-bit bitvectors indexed by     │

           │         ```                                                  │
           │         """                                                  │
           │         pass                                                 │
           │                                                              │
           │     def outputs(self):                                       │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the output variables and their │
           │ types.                                                       │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines an │
           │ output variable y,                                           │
           │         which is a real number:                              │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         def outputs(self):                                   │
           │             self.y = Real()                                  │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         """                                                  │
           │         pass                                                 │
           │                                                              │

           │         ```                                                  │
           │         """                                                  │
           │         pass                                                 │
           │                                                              │

           │         ```                                                  │
           │         """                                                  │
           │         pass                                                 │

           │         greater than or equal to 20:                         │

           │         """                                                  │

           │         """                                                  │

           │         """(Optional) Defines the type declarations.         │

           │         pass                                                 │

           │ types.                                                       │

           │ 8-bit variable x                                             │
           │         and an integer variable y:                           │

           │         ```                                                  │
           │         """                                                  │

           │                                                              │

           │         which is an array of 8-bit bitvectors indexed by     │

           │         ```                                                  │
           │         """                                                  │
           │         pass                                                 │
           │                                                              │
           │     def outputs(self):                                       │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the output variables and their │

           │ output variable y,                                           │
           │         which is a real number:                              │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         def outputs(self):                                   │
           │             self.y = Real()                                  │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         """                                                  │
           │         pass                                                 │
           │                                                              │
           │     def shared_vars(self):                                   │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the shared variables and their │

           │ shared variable z,                                           │

           │         ```                                                  │
           │         def shared_vars(self):                               │
           │             self.z = Array(Integer(), Boolean())             │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         """                                                  │
           │         pass                                                 │
           │                                                              │
           │     def instances(self):                                     │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the instances of other modules │
           │ and relates their                                            │
           │         input, output, and shared variables to local         │
           │ variables. Every instance                                    │

           │         implementation defines an instance of M called m,    │

           │         ```                                                  │
           │         """                                                  │

           │                                                              │

           │ initializes x to 0 if y is                                   │
           │         greater than or equal to 20:                         │
           │         ```                                                  │
           │         def init(self):                                      │

           │         """                                                  │

           │ block: specification                                         │
           │ fix: The invariant for L1 (L1_inv), which is                 │
           │ 'Implies(self.L1, (self.L3 and (self.L2 and self.L4)))'      │
           │ doesn't match the intended functionality. The traffic lights │
           │ are supposed to turn on in a specific order, but this        │
           │ invariant suggests that if L1 is True, all others (L2, L3,   │
           │ L4) must also be true, which contradicts the specified order │
           │ where L1 should be paired with L3, and then L2 alone,        │
           │ followed by L4. The invariant should only require L3 with    │
           │ L1, not all lights.                                          │
           │                                                              │
           │ block: specification                                         │
           │ fix: Additionally, there seems to be confusion in the logic  │
           │ for L4_inv and the transition ordering. L4_inv is currently  │
           │ '(self.L4 or (self.L4 == self.amber))', meaning L4 can only  │
           │ be True if amber is True. This should instead align with the │
           │ exclusive times L4 is active (after L2).                     │

           │ state machine through yellow and red accordingly before      │
           │ moving to green for L2.                                      │
